# Waterdrop prompt bank v1: 800 unique scenes, one seed per prompt.
# Format: <prompt> | <target> | <effect>

A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, a calm shallow pond is completely still, with a perfectly flat water surface. No falling droplet, splash, impact cavity, or ripple is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center of the water surface. Only after contact, a small crown splash rises and concentric ripples spread outward. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small crown splash rises and concentric ripples spread outward
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, a calm shallow pond is completely still, with a calm level water surface. No falling droplet, splash, impact cavity, or ripple is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly left of the center of the water surface. Only after contact, a brief impact cavity closes and circular ripples cross the surface. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a brief impact cavity closes and circular ripples cross the surface
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, a calm shallow pond is completely still, with a shallow motionless water surface. No falling droplet, splash, impact cavity, or ripple is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly right of center. Only after contact, a low radial splash forms and several shallow ripples expand outward. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a low radial splash forms and several shallow ripples expand outward
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, a calm shallow pond is completely still, with the water surface and its nearest edge clearly visible. No falling droplet, splash, impact cavity, or ripple is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts near the visible inner edge of the water surface. Only after contact, an asymmetric splash forms and curved ripples travel toward the opposite edge. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an asymmetric splash forms and curved ripples travel toward the opposite edge

Only after contact, an asymmetric splash forms and curved ripples travel toward the opposite edge. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an asymmetric splash forms and curved ripples travel toward the opposite edge
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, a stainless-steel pet water bowl is completely still, with a perfectly flat water surface. No falling droplet, splash, impact cavity, or ripple is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center of the water surface. Only after contact, a small crown splash rises and concentric ripples spread outward. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small crown splash rises and concentric ripples spread outward

The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a brief impact cavity closes and circular ripples cross the surface

Only after contact, a small splash appears and a thin localized wet spot remains. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small splash appears and a thin localized wet spot remains

Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center. Only after contact, a brief radial splash appears and several clear water beads remain. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a brief radial splash appears and several clear water beads remain

No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly left of center. A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, rigid plastic cafeteria tray is clean, dry, horizontal, and covered by a hydrophobic coating. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly right of center. Only after contact, the water recoils after a tiny splash and gathers into one compact bead. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | the water recoils after a tiny splash and gathers into one compact bead
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, rigid plastic cafeteria tray is clean, completely dry, horizontal, and framed with one edge clearly visible. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts near the visible edge. Only after contact, an asymmetric splash appears and a small amount of water gathers against the edge. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an asymmetric splash appears and a small amount of water gathers against the edge

The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an asymmetric wet patch appears and slowly spreads inward from the contact point

The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an asymmetric wet patch appears and slowly spreads inward from the contact point
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, unpainted gypsum board is clean, completely dry, flat, and motionless. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center. Only after contact, the water is absorbed and a small dark wet patch gradually expands. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center. Only after contact, the water is absorbed and a small dark wet patch gradually expands. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | the water is absorbed and a small dark wet patch gradually expands
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, unfinished poplar board is clean, completely dry, flat, and supported on a rigid surface. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly left of center. Only after contact, the water soaks in and leaves a compact dark damp spot with little outward spread. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | the water soaks in and leaves a compact dark damp spot with little outward spread
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, unfinished poplar board is clean, completely dry, flat, and showing a clear grain or fiber direction. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly right of center. Only after contact, an elongated wet mark gradually spreads along the visible grain or fibers. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an elongated wet mark gradually spreads along the visible grain or fibers
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, unfinished poplar board is clean, completely dry, flat, and firmly supported so it cannot move. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts near one visible edge. Only after contact, an asymmetric wet patch appears and slowly spreads inward from the contact point. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | an asymmetric wet patch appears and slowly spreads inward from the contact point

A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, fine dry sand is arranged as a smooth level bed and completely undisturbed. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center of the bed. Only after contact, a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, fine dry sand is arranged as a small dry mound and completely undisturbed. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the top of the mound. Only after contact, a small indentation forms, several particles scatter down the sides, and a damp clump remains. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small indentation forms, several particles scatter down the sides, and a damp clump remains
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, fine dry sand is arranged as a lightly compacted dry layer and completely undisturbed. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly off-center. Only after contact, a shallow dent forms and nearby particles bind into a small darker damp cluster. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a shallow dent forms and nearby particles bind into a small darker damp cluster

The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp

Only after contact, a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, fine dry breadcrumbs is arranged as a small dry mound and completely undisturbed. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the top of the mound. Only after contact, a small indentation forms, several particles scatter down the sides, and a damp clump remains. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small indentation forms, several particles scatter down the sides, and a damp clump remains

Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the center of the bed. Only after contact, a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small crater forms, a few particles move outward, and the center becomes darker and damp
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, dry chia seeds is arranged as a small dry mound and completely undisturbed. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts the top of the mound. Only after contact, a small indentation forms, several particles scatter down the sides, and a damp clump remains. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a small indentation forms, several particles scatter down the sides, and a damp clump remains
A realistic fixed-camera macro video in one continuous shot. During the first two seconds, dry chia seeds is arranged as a lightly compacted dry layer and completely undisturbed. No water, falling droplet, or wet footprint is visible during these first two seconds. Then exactly one large clear water droplet enters from the top of the frame, falls visibly downward, and contacts slightly off-center. Only after contact, a shallow dent forms and nearby particles bind into a small darker damp cluster. The camera, lighting, background, receiver geometry, and all other objects remain unchanged. | single falling water droplet | a shallow dent forms and nearby particles bind into a small darker damp cluster
